# Lean proof index

Every named `theorem` and `lemma` declaration under `the-final-letter/topics/**/*.lean`, with a link to the declaration line and a LaTeX rendering of its statement.

**Coverage:** 52 topic folders · 120 Lean files · **2,310 named proofs** (1,987 theorems, 323 lemmas) · 19 anonymous `example` proofs.

**Visualisations:** [concept graph](lean-graph.html) · [proof–conjecture graph](lean-conjecture-graph.html).

Generated by [`tools/build_lean_index.py`](tools/build_lean_index.py). Regenerate with:

```bash
python3 the-final-letter/topics/tools/build_lean_index.py
```

## How to read this index

- Each row links to the **line of the declaration** in its Lean file.
- Scope: named `theorem` and `lemma` declarations. `def`, `instance`, `abbrev`, `structure` and `inductive` bodies are definitions rather than named proofs, so they are excluded; anonymous `example`s are listed separately.
- Comments and string literals are stripped before scanning, so prose mentioning a theorem is not counted.
- The **Statement (LaTeX)** column is a mechanical rendering of the Lean type, not a hand-edited mathematical statement. Binders become `\forall` clauses, identifiers are set in `\mathrm`, and Lean-specific syntax such as `∫ ... in ...` is kept literal. The Lean source is authoritative.
- The index records names, statements and locations. It does not re-run Lean; see each topic's `lean/check.sh` for its build.
- A comment-stripped scan found **no `sorry`, `admit` or `axiom`** in these files.

| [552](absolute-polarized-hodge-index-realization/lean/Hadamard.lean#L552) | theorem | `Jensen_finiteCanonicalProduct` | $\forall\,p : \mathbb{N},\ \forall\,S : \mathrm{Finset}\ \mathbb{C},\ \forall\,\_h_{0} : (0:\mathbb{C})\ \notin\ S,\ \forall\,R : \mathbb{R},\ \forall\,hR : R\ne\ 0,\ \mathrm{Real.circleAverage}(\lambda\ z\Rightarrow\ \mathrm{Real.log}\ \Vert{}\mathrm{finiteCanonicalProduct}\ p\ S\ z\Vert)\ 0\ R=\sum{}^{f}\ u:\mathbb{C},(\mathrm{MeromorphicOn.divisor}(\mathrm{finiteCanonicalProduct}\ p\ S)(\mathrm{Metric.closedBall}\ 0\ \vert{}R\vert))\ u\cdot\ \mathrm{Real.log}(R\cdot\ \Vert(0:\mathbb{C})-u\Vert^{-1})+\mathrm{Real.log}\ \Vert{}\mathrm{finiteCanonicalProduct}\ p\ S\ 0\Vert$ |

| [547](euler-maclaurin/lean/Topic.lean#L547) | lemma | `global_recursion_raw` | $\forall\,n : \mathbb{N},\ \forall\,hn : 1\le\ n,\ \forall\,f : \mathbb{R}\to\ \mathbb{C},\ \forall\,hf : \mathrm{ContDiff}\ \mathbb{R}(\uparrow(\top:\mathbb{N}\infty))\ f,\ \forall\,M\,N : \mathbb{N},\ \forall\,hMN : M\le\ N,\ (((n+1:\mathbb{N}):\mathbb{C})\cdot((n+2:\mathbb{N}):\mathbb{C}))\cdot(\int\ x\ \text{in}(M:\mathbb{R})..(N:\mathbb{R}),(\mathrm{periodicBernoulli}\ n\ x:\mathbb{C})\cdot\ \mathrm{iteratedDeriv}\ n\ f\ x)=(((n+2:\mathbb{N}):\mathbb{C})\cdot((_{\mathrm{root}_{.\mathrm{bernoulli}}}(n+1):\mathbb{R}):\mathbb{C}))\cdot(\mathrm{iteratedDeriv}\ n\ f(N:\mathbb{R})-\mathrm{iteratedDeriv}\ n\ f(M:\mathbb{R}))-(((_{\mathrm{root}_{.\mathrm{bernoulli}}}(n+2):\mathbb{R}):\mathbb{C})\cdot(\mathrm{iteratedDeriv}(n+1)\ f(N:\mathbb{R})-\mathrm{iteratedDeriv}(n+1)\ f(M:\mathbb{R})))+(\int\ x\ \text{in}(M:\mathbb{R})..(N:\mathbb{R}),(\mathrm{periodicBernoulli}(n+2)\ x:\mathbb{C})\cdot\ \mathrm{iteratedDeriv}(n+2)\ f\ x)$ |

| [547](partial-sum-plus-tail/lean/EulerMaclaurin.lean#L547) | lemma | `global_recursion_raw` | $\forall\,n : \mathbb{N},\ \forall\,hn : 1\le\ n,\ \forall\,f : \mathbb{R}\to\ \mathbb{C},\ \forall\,hf : \mathrm{ContDiff}\ \mathbb{R}(\uparrow(\top:\mathbb{N}\infty))\ f,\ \forall\,M\,N : \mathbb{N},\ \forall\,hMN : M\le\ N,\ (((n+1:\mathbb{N}):\mathbb{C})\cdot((n+2:\mathbb{N}):\mathbb{C}))\cdot(\int\ x\ \text{in}(M:\mathbb{R})..(N:\mathbb{R}),(\mathrm{periodicBernoulli}\ n\ x:\mathbb{C})\cdot\ \mathrm{iteratedDeriv}\ n\ f\ x)=(((n+2:\mathbb{N}):\mathbb{C})\cdot((_{\mathrm{root}_{.\mathrm{bernoulli}}}(n+1):\mathbb{R}):\mathbb{C}))\cdot(\mathrm{iteratedDeriv}\ n\ f(N:\mathbb{R})-\mathrm{iteratedDeriv}\ n\ f(M:\mathbb{R}))-(((_{\mathrm{root}_{.\mathrm{bernoulli}}}(n+2):\mathbb{R}):\mathbb{C})\cdot(\mathrm{iteratedDeriv}(n+1)\ f(N:\mathbb{R})-\mathrm{iteratedDeriv}(n+1)\ f(M:\mathbb{R})))+(\int\ x\ \text{in}(M:\mathbb{R})..(N:\mathbb{R}),(\mathrm{periodicBernoulli}(n+2)\ x:\mathbb{C})\cdot\ \mathrm{iteratedDeriv}(n+2)\ f\ x)$ |
